## 概率核心密度如何起作用 · 概率核心密度如何起作用

**id**　ce6bb06cb37ddd43f1-1

**english**　How Probabilistic Kernel Density works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間分析員工具箱
- 密度工具
- 密度密度工具裝置概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**text**　概率心核密度工具可以使用空間分析員的許可證。 概率核心密度工具在計算該估計數的不確定性時,估計點特征的密度。 該工具重復了一種或多種不確定來源下規定的估計次數。 它與傳統的密度分析不同, 因為將輸入量視為不確定。 它不但沒有生成單一密度估計, 而是反復重新計算密度, 同時也考慮到點重量、 點位置或抽樣一組點的不確定性。 然后該工具將模擬結果匯總為平均密度表面和不確定性測量尺度, 用以描述該估計數的可變性性。 這樣不僅能夠評估高密度地區, 還可以評估您對結果的信心程度。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Available with the Spatial Analyst license.\nThe Probabilistic Kernel Density tool estimates the density of point features while quantifying the uncertainty of that estimate. The tool repeats the estimate specified amount of times under one or more sources of uncertainty. It differs from traditional density analysis because it treats the inputs as uncertain. Rather than generating a single density estimate, it repeatedly recalculates density while accounting for uncertainty in point weights, point locations, or the sampled set of points. The tool then summarizes the simulation results into a mean density surface and uncertainty measures that describe the variability of the estimate. This allows you to evaluate not only where density is high, but also how confident you can be in that result.", "section": "How Probabilistic Kernel Density works"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

## 概率核心密度如何起作用 · 概率核心密度惠益

**id**　ce6bb06cb37ddd43f1-2

**english**　How Probabilistic Kernel Density works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間分析員工具箱
- 密度工具
- 密度密度工具裝置概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#JBG

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**text**　概率心核密度估計工具將內核密度估計與基于模擬的不確定性傳播結合起來(Heuvelink,1998年)。 與傳統的密度估計相比,它具有以下好處:產生平均密度表面,可選擇性地產生標準偏差、變異系數和超常概率斜線。這些產出有助于量化密度估計中的不確定性。區分由少量不確定觀測驅動的模式的穩定密度模式。這可以支持更知情的決策。查明哪些濃度穩定,哪些是幾個不確定點的文物,支持比較容易辨別的決定。為模型確定不確定性提供靈活性。您可以配置不確定性的類型、抽樣方法、取樣策略、屬性錯誤模型和分布。符合資料和分析要求的方法。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The Probabilistic Kernel Density tool combines kernel density estimation with simulation-based uncertainty propagation (Heuvelink, 1998). Compared to a traditional density estimate, it offers the following benefits:\nProduces a mean density surface and can optionally generate standard deviation, coefficient of variation, and exceedance probability rasters. These outputs help quantify uncertainty in the density estimate.\nDistinguishes stable density patterns from patterns driven by a small number of uncertain observations. This can support more informed decision-making.\nIdentifies which concentrations are stable and which are artifacts of a few uncertain points, supporting more defensible decisions.\nProvides flexibility for modeling uncertainty. You can configure the uncertainty type, sampling method, sampling strategy, attribute error model, and distribution method to match your data and analytical requirements.", "section": "Benefits of Probabilistic Kernel Density"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#JBG

## 概率核心密度如何起作用 · 計算概率內核密度

**id**　ce6bb06cb37ddd43f1-3

**english**　How Probabilistic Kernel Density works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間分析員工具箱
- 密度工具
- 密度密度工具裝置概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#JJQ

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**text**　從概念上講,該工具適合每個點的平滑內核表面,類似于核心密度工具。內核表面以Silverman(1986年,第76頁)中描述的孔內核函數為基礎。對于每一個輸出單元格,密度值計算為該單元格中心所有內核表面數值的總和,其公式如下:(w_i = 1\) 當沒有指定人口欄位參數值時。 默認情況下, 它為無 。\( h\) 是地圖單位中的帶寬( 搜索半徑) 。\( K\) 是內核函數 。\( max) \\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\s- x_i\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\ \\\ \ \ \ \\ \ \\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ ( s\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 事件 \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 。 。 概率核心中樞 \\ \ \ \ \ \\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ 。 \ \ \ 在所有模擬完成后,對產生的表面進行總結,以產生輸出弧體。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Conceptually, the tool fits a smooth kernel surface over each point, similar to the Kernel Density tool. The kernel surface is based on the quartic kernel function described in Silverman (1986, p. 76).\nFor each output cell, the density value is calculated as the sum of all kernel surface values at the center of that cell with the following formula:\nwhere: \\(\\large \\hat{\\lambda}_h(s)\\) is the estimated density at location \\(s\\) using bandwidth \\(h\\). \\(n\\) is the number of event points. \\(w_i\\) is the weight of event \\(i\\), where \\(w_i = 1\\) when no Population Field parameter value is specified. It will be NONE by default. \\(h\\) is the bandwidth (search radius) in map units. \\(K\\) is the kernel function. \\(\\large |s-x_i|\\) is the distance from location \\(s\\) to event \\(i\\).\nThe Probabilistic Kernel Density tool extends this estimate through simulation. For each simulation, the inputs are randomly adjusted to reflect the selected sources of uncertainty These adjustments, referred to as perturbations, may affect point locations, point weights, or the sampled set of points before a kernel density surface is calculated. After all simulations are complete, the resulting surfaces are summarized to generate the output rasters.", "section": "Calculate probabilistic kernel density"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#JJQ

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Output density value from a single simulation is calculated using the following formula:\nwhere: \\(\\hat{\\lambda}^{b}_h(s)\\) is the estimated density at location \\(s\\) using bandwidth \\(h\\) in simulation \\(b\\). \\(n_b\\) is the number of points used in simulation \\(b\\) (equal to \\(n\\) unless subsampling is used). \\(w_i^{(b)}\\) is the weight of point \\(i\\) in simulation \\(b\\) after attribute perturbation. \\(x_i^{(b)}\\) is the location of point \\(i\\) in simulation \\(b\\) after any location perturbation. \\(h\\) is the bandwidth (search radius) in map units. \\(K\\) is the kernel function. \\(\\large |s-x_i^{b}|\\) is the distance from location \\(s\\) to point \\(i\\) in simulation \\(b\\).\nA single run proceeds in two stages. ## 概率核心密度如何起作用 · 模擬階段

**id**　ce6bb06cb37ddd43f1-5

**english**　How Probabilistic Kernel Density works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間分析員工具箱
- 密度工具
- 密度密度工具裝置概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#CJK

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**text**　\ (B\) 每個模擬都根據選定的不確定類型對輸入進行擾動,并從受擾動的投入中計算內核密度表面。以下三個可用的不確定性類型參數值可以單獨使用或組合使用。當選擇多個選項時,每個模擬都應用相應的擾動。抽樣:此類型代表了取樣的不確定性。事件經過重新標注,因此每個模擬都使用了略微不同的事件樣本。位置:該類型代表著定位不確定性。每個事件的位置都被從正常分布中隨機提取的數量所抵消。遷移的標準偏差以位置偏移系數參數值乘以搜索半徑參數值來計算。屬性:此類型代表不確定性。每一重量都是根據差幅或固定百分比確定的分布線重新劃分的,而該分布線的差幅則由誤差幅度或固定百分比確定。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Each of the \\(B\\) simulations perturbs the inputs according to the selected uncertainty types and computes a kernel density surface from the perturbed inputs.\nThe three available Uncertainty Type parameter values described below can be used individually or in combination. When multiple options are selected, all corresponding perturbations are applied within each simulation.\nSampling: This type represents sampling uncertainty. The events are resampled, so each simulation uses a slightly different sample of events.\nLocation: This type represents positional uncertainty. Each event location is offset by a random amount drawn from a normal distribution. The standard deviation of the displacement is calculated as the Location Displacement Factor parameter value multiplied by the Search Radius parameter value.\nAttribute: This type represents uncertainty in the population-field weights. Each weight is redrawn from a distribution whose spread is defined by either a margin of error or a fixed percentage.", "section": "Simulation stage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#CJK

**id**　ce6bb06cb37ddd43f1-6

**english**　How Probabilistic Kernel Density works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具

- 密度工具

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#DXH

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**text**　模擬完成后,工具為每個輸出單元格提供一套密度值。這些值代表該位置的密度估計分布。這些值的平均值是主密度光柵中的細胞值。它們的變異性是標準差中的單元格值和變異輸出弧體的系數。超常概率光柵記錄了超過特定閾值的模擬密度值比例。由于這些摘要直接根據模擬值計算,因此沒有就分布形狀做出假設。下圖顯示了單個單元格模擬密度值分布情況的實例。平均密度值成為主密度弧體中的細胞值,而分配的分布則用來計算不確定性。弧度。三種不確定類型在它們擾動時的不同。屬性不確定類型改變加權數,地點不確定類型改變位置,抽樣不確定類型改變分析中使用的一組點。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "After the simulations are complete, the tool has a set of density values for each output cell. These values represent the distribution of the density estimate at that location.\nThe mean of these values becomes the cell value in the primary density raster. Their variability becomes the cell values in the standard deviation and coefficient of variation output rasters. The exceedance probability raster records the proportion of simulated density values that exceed a specified threshold.\nBecause these summaries are calculated directly from the simulated values, no assumptions are made about the shape of the distribution.\nThe graph below shows an example of the distribution of simulated density values for a single cell. The mean density value becomes the cell value in the primary density raster, and the spread of the distribution is used to calculate the uncertainty rasters.\nThe three uncertainty types differ in what they perturb. The Attribute uncertainty type changes the weights, the Location uncertainty type changes the positions, and the Sampling uncertainty type changes the set of points used in the analysis.", "section": "Summary stage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#DXH

## 概率核心密度如何起作用 · 取樣不確定性

**id**　ce6bb06cb37ddd43f1-8

**english**　How Probabilistic Kernel Density works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間分析員工具箱
- 密度工具
- 密度密度工具裝置概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#Z57

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**text**　抽樣不確定性代表了以下事實:所觀測到的點只是基礎人口或過程的樣本。不同的樣本可能產生不同的密度表面。為了說明這種不確定性,每個模擬重劃點時使用選定的取樣戰略和取樣方法參數值(Efron,1979年)。資料對抽樣參數的百分比控制著重新標注資料集的大小。抽樣戰略和采樣方法參數控制著如何進行再標選。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Sampling uncertainty represents the fact that the observed points are only a sample of the underlying population or process. A different sample could produce a different density surface. To account for this uncertainty, each simulation redraws the points using the selected Sampling Strategy and Sampling Method parameter values (Efron, 1979). The Percentage of Data to Sample parameter controls the size of the resampled dataset. The Sampling Strategy and Sampling Method parameters control how the resampling is performed.", "section": "Sampling uncertainty"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#Z57

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**text**　這是默認的取樣方法。 在每次模擬中,\( n) 點會與原始的一套點數( Efron, 1979年) 替換而繪制 \ (n) 。 因此, 一些點可能會被多次選擇, 而其他點可能根本不可能被選中。 此方法提供了一個公正的基線 。 隨著模擬數量的增加, 抽樣錯誤會比 \ (1 /\ sqrt{B}} (Metropolis and Ulam, 1949年) 減少大約比例。 它應用了以下公式 :\\ (\\\ max_ i\ (b)  ⁇ i=1\\ n}~ 以\\ (x_ 1) 代替的樣本 \\\ ldots\\\ (x_ 1)\ reample, x_n ⁇ ) :\(x_i}(b) ⁇ ) 是模擬\ (b\) 中繪制的點。\ ( ⁇ x_ 1,\ ldots, x_n ⁇ ) 是原始的一組點 。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "This is the default sampling method. In each simulation, \\(n\\) points are drawn with replacements from the original set of \\(N\\) points (Efron, 1979). As a result, some points may be selected multiple times, while others may not be selected at all.\nThis approach provides an unbiased baseline. As the number of simulations increases, the sampling error decreases approximately in proportion to \\(1 / \\sqrt{B}\\) (Metropolis and Ulam, 1949).\nIt applies the following formula:\n\\({\\large \\{x_i^{(b)}\\}_{i=1}^{n}}\\) ~ resample with replacement from \\(\\{x_1, \\ldots, x_n\\}\\)\nwhere: \\(x_i^{(b)}\\) is the point drawn in simulation \\(b\\). \\(\\{x_1, \\ldots, x_n\\}\\) is the original set of points.", "section": "Random sampling"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#SW1

**id**　ce6bb06cb37ddd43f1-12

**english**　How Probabilistic Kernel Density works

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#P08

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**text**　子抽樣抽取一組較小的\(m)分點,而不替換(Politis、Romano和Wolf,1999年)。次樣本大小由資料對抽樣參數的百分比決定。為每次模擬選擇的點數使用以下公式計算:\(grand m =\ llop \ pn\\ rfloor\),其中:\(m\) 是子樣本大小。\(p)是資料對抽樣參數的百分比指定的部分。\(n)是輸入點的數目。然后通過選擇\(m)獲得模擬的子樣本。\(b\)隨機點數,不替換原始的 & (n\) 組合點數。 子抽樣由以下公式表示 :\ (\\ large S ⁇ (b)}\ subset X,\ qquad {S ⁇ (b)\\ = m\) , 其中 :\ (S\\ (b)\\ ) 是模擬所選的子抽樣 \ (b\) 。\ (m\) 是子抽樣大小 。\ (X\) 是全套輸入點 。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Subsampling draws a smaller set of \\(m\\) distinct points without replacement (Politis, Romano, and Wolf, 1999). The subsample size is determined by the Percentage of Data to Sample parameter.\nThe number of points selected for each simulation is calculated using the following formula:\n\\(\\large m = \\lfloor \\ pn \\ \\rfloor\\)\nwhere: \\(m\\) is the subsample size. \\(p\\) is the fraction specified by the Percentage of Data to Sample parameter. \\(n\\) is the number of input points.\nThe subsample for simulation \\(b\\) is then obtained by selecting \\(m\\) points at random without replacement from the original set of \\(n\\) points.\nThe subsample is represented by the following formula:\n\\(\\large S^{(b)} \\subset X, \\qquad |S^{(b)}| = m\\)\nwhere: \\(S^{(b)}\\) is the subsample selected for simulation \\(b\\). \\(m\\) is the subsample size. \\(X\\) is the full set of input points.", "section": "Subsampling"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#P08

## 概率核心密度如何起作用 · 最遠點取樣

**id**　ce6bb06cb37ddd43f1-14

**english**　How Probabilistic Kernel Density works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#GEI

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**text**　Farthest 點抽樣選擇盡可能相距最遠的點。 每一步, 它會通過應用以下公式來添加最接近已經選定的鄰里最遠的點( Eldar 等人,1997年) :\ (\\ maxx@ k+1} =\ large~ max\\ max\ in X\ setminus S_k} \ min ⁇ s\ in S_ k ⁇ \ grand {x - s ⁇ ) , 其中 :\ (S_ k\\) 是步驟后選中的一組點 。\ (X\) 是完整的一組點 。\ (xk+1}) 是下一個選中的點 。\ (x)\\ k+1\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\,選擇將其與當前選擇的距離最大化。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Farthest point sampling selects points that are as far apart as possible. At each step, it adds the point whose nearest already-selected neighbor is most distant (Eldar et al., 1997) by applying the following formula:\n\\(\\large x_{k+1} = \\Large{\\arg\\max_{x \\in X \\setminus S_k} \\min_{s \\in S_k}} \\large \\|x - s\\|\\)\nwhere: \\(S_k\\) is the set of points selected after step \\(k\\). \\(X\\) is the full set of points. \\(x_{k+1}\\) is the next point selected, chosen to maximize its distance from the current selection.", "section": "Farthest point sampling"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#GEI

## 概率核心密度如何起作用 · 提示 :

**id**　ce6bb06cb37ddd43f1-15

**english**　How Probabilistic Kernel Density works

**chapter**

**text**　使用取樣戰略參數中最遠點抽樣值以最大限度地擴大空間覆蓋范圍和減少集群。 由于點選擇幾乎是決定性的,它可能低估了采樣不確定性。

**text**　取樣方法參數控制重新抽樣的點是否保留在原有位置或空間調整。標準布設參數選項重新使用準確點位置。滑布設參數選項在每個重新抽樣點上添加一個小內核形狀變位(Silverman and Young,1987年)。這一調整有助于減少在同一地點重復放置點數。在稀少資料集中特別有用,否則選擇的多倍點數將保持完全相同的座標。每個重標點的移位數使用以下方程式計算:\ (\) Large_x_i ⁇ = x_j+ h_s\epsilon}在那里:\ (x)_i\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\(x_j\)\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\(h\\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\c\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\隨機的移移。\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The Sampling Method parameter controls whether resampled points remain at their original locations or are spatially adjusted. The Standard Bootstrap parameter option reuses the exact point locations. The Smooth Bootstrap parameter option adds a small kernel-shaped displacement to each resampled point (Silverman and Young, 1987). This adjustment helps reduce repeated placement of points at the same location. It can be particularly useful in sparse datasets, where a point selected multiple times would otherwise remain at exactly the same coordinates.\nThe displaced location of each resampled point is calculated using the following equation:\n\\(\\Large{{x_i}^{*} = x_j + h_s \\epsilon}\\)\nwhere: \\(x_i^{*}\\) is the smoothed-bootstrap event location. \\(x_j\\) is the resampled event location. \\(h_s\\) is the kernel-noise bandwidth, proportional to \\(h\\). \\(\\large \\epsilon\\) is the random displacement drawn from kernel \\(K\\).", "section": "Standard versus smooth bootstrap"}

**text**　位置不確定性代表了各個點的位置錯誤, 如地理編碼位置、 GPS 修正或者不確切的子體。 每次模擬都用從雙變量正態分布和重新估計中隨機抵消的方式取代每個點。 位置移位系數參數控制點移位有多遠, 以搜索半徑值的一個部分表示 。 選擇一個反映輸入資料位置精確度的值。 每個點使用以下方程式被移位 :\ (\ max{ x_i}= x_i +\delta_ i}, 其中 :\\\\\\ delta_ i\) 是事件( i\) 應用的隨機位置移位偏法 。\ (\ delta_ i\ \ sim N\ lft( 0, \ sigma=2 I_ 2\right)\) 在每個方向上都是雙變量正態分布, 平均 0 和差異 \ (\\\\\ gma_ 2\\ ) 。 矩陣\ ( I_ 2\ \ ) 是 \ ( 2\ title 2\ ) 身份矩陣 。\ (\\\\ \\ \\\ \\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ ) 是位置錯誤尺度, 計算為位置移位系數乘以\ (h\) 。\ (x_ i\ \ ) 是事件記錄的位置 \ (i\) 。\ (x_ i\ \) 是事件的移位位置 \ (i\ \ ) 。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Location uncertainty represents positional error in the points, such as a geocoded location, a GPS fix, or a centroid that is not exactly where the phenomenon is. Each simulation displaces every point by a random offset drawn from a bivariate normal distribution and re-estimates. The Location Displacement Factor parameter controls how far points are displaced, expressed as a fraction of the Search Radius value. Choose a value that reflects the positional accuracy of the input data.\nEach point is displaced using the following equation:\n\\(\\large{x_i^{*} = x_i + \\delta_i}\\)\nwhere: \\(\\delta_i\\) is the random positional offset applied to event \\(i\\). where: \\(\\delta_i \\sim N\\left(0, \\sigma^2 I_2\\right)\\) It is a bivariate normal distribution with mean 0 and variance \\(\\sigma^2\\) in each direction. The matrix \\(I_2\\) is the \\(2 \\times 2\\) identity matrix. \\(\\sigma\\) is the positional error scale, calculated as the Location Displacement Factor value multiplied by \\(h\\). \\(x_i\\) is the recorded location of event \\(i\\). \\(x_i^{*}\\) is the displaced location of event \\(i\\).", "section": "Location uncertainty"}

**content_version**　3.8

**original**　{"text": "The Normal distribution option redraws weights using a normal distribution centered on the original value.\nThe perturbed weight is calculated using the following formula:\n\\(w_i^{*} = w_i + SE_i \\cdot Z, \\quad Z \\sim N(0, 1)\\)\nwhere: \\(w_i^{*}\\) is the perturbed weight for event \\(i\\). \\(w_i\\) is the original weight for event \\(i\\). \\(SE_i\\) is the standard error of the weight. \\(Z\\) is a standard normal random variable with mean 0 and variance 1.\nFor example, with \\(w = 500\\), \\(SE = 50\\), and \\(Z = 1.2\\), the perturbed weight is \\(w^{*} = 500 + 1.2 \\times 50 = 560\\).", "section": "Normal"}

**original**　{"text": "The Lognormal option is right-skewed and strictly positive, making it well suited for count data and other nonnegative quantities (Limpert, Stahel, and Abbt, 2001).\nThe perturbed weight is calculated using the following formula:\n\\(\\large{w_i^{*} = w_i \\times \\exp\\left(\\sigma_{\\ln} Z - \\frac{1}{2}\\sigma_{\\ln}^2\\right)}\\)\nwhere: \\(w_i^{*}\\) is the perturbed weight for event \\(i\\) \\(w_i\\) is the original weight for event \\(i\\) \\(\\sigma_{\\ln}\\) is the lognormal shape parameter derived. where \\(\\large{\\sigma_{\\ln} = \\sqrt{\\ln\\left(1 + \\left(\\frac{SE_i}{w_i}\\right)^2\\right)}}\\) It is derived from \\(SE_i\\) and \\(w_i\\). \\(SE_i\\) is the standard error of the weight \\(Z\\) is a standard normal random variable with mean 0 and variance 1\nFor example, with \\(w = 500\\), \\(SE = 50\\), and \\(Z = 1.2\\), the lognormal shape parameter is \\(\\sigma_{\\ln} = 0.0998\\). The perturbed weight is \\(w^{*} \\approx 561\\).", "section": "Lognormal"}

**id**　ce6bb06cb37ddd43f1-27

**english**　How Probabilistic Kernel Density works

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#S37

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**text**　統一選項從以原始值為中心的對稱間隔中抽取加權數。 間隔內的所有數值都同樣可能。 擾動加權數使用以下公式計算:\ (\ ragent{w_ i}\; w_ i+ a_ i,\; w_ i+ a_ i),\ quad a_ i= 3SE_ i}, 其中 \ (w_ i) 是事件\( i\\ \ \ \ \ \ ) 的周期性加權數。\ (w_ i\) \ (w) 是事件的原始加權數 \ (i\) 。\ (Se_ i\) 是重量的標準錯誤 。\ (a_ i\)是間隔的半角, 等于\( 3SE_ i\ ) 。 例如, 以\ (w) = 500\ 和\ (SE = 50\ ) 來計算, 間隔的半角是 \ (a = 150\ ) 。 周期性重量從\ ([350 650]\ 中統一抽取。 如果模擬跨間隔0. 0. 70, 周期性重量是 \ (w) = 350 + 0.70\ 時間(650 - 350) =560\ )。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The Uniform option draws weights from a symmetric interval centered on the original value. All values within the interval are equally likely.\nThe perturbed weight is calculated using the following formula:\n\\(\\large{w_i^{*} \\sim U(w_i - a_i,\\; w_i + a_i), \\quad a_i = 3SE_i}\\)\nwhere: \\(w_i^{*}\\) is the perturbed weight for event \\(i\\). \\(w_i\\) is the original weight for event \\(i\\). \\(SE_i\\) is the standard error of the weight. \\(a_i\\) is the half-width of the interval, equal to \\(3SE_i\\).\nFor example, with \\(w = 500\\) and \\(SE = 50\\), the half-width of the interval is \\(a = 150\\). The perturbed weight is drawn uniformly from \\([350, 650]\\). If a simulation draws 0.70 of the way across the interval, the perturbed weight is \\(w^{*} = 350 + 0.70 \\times (650 - 350) = 560\\).", "section": "Uniform"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**id**　ce6bb06cb37ddd43f1-28

**english**　How Probabilistic Kernel Density works

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**text**　三角選項從以原始值為中心的對稱三角分布中抽取重量。 原值的可能性最大, 概率則直線向極限遞減。 擾動重量使用以下公式計算 :\ (\ raig{ w_i}\\ sim Tri( w_i - c_i,\; w_i+ c_i),\ quad c_i =\ sqrt{6}, SE_ i ⁇ ) , 其中 :\ (w_ i}) 是事件 \ (i\) 的周期性重量。\ (w_ i\) \ (w_i\) 是事件的初始重量 \ (i\) \ (SE_) 。i) 是重量的標準錯誤。\ (c_ i\) 是分布的半角, 等于\( sqrt{ 6\\\, SE_ i\) 。 例如, 以\( w= 500\) 和\( SE= 50\, 半邊是\( c = \ sqrt{ 6}\ times 50\ approx 122.5\ ) 。 被擾動的重量來自間隔的三角分布 \( [377. 5, 622.5\ ] ) , 最高概率為 500 。 一個模擬可以繪制 \ (w\ = 560\ )。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The Triangular option draws weights from a symmetric triangular distribution centered on the original value. The original value is most likely, and the probability decreases linearly toward the limits.\nThe perturbed weight is calculated using the following formula:\n\\(\\large{w_i^{*} \\sim Tri(w_i - c_i,\\; w_i + c_i), \\quad c_i = \\sqrt{6}\\,SE_i}\\)\nwhere: \\(w_i^{*}\\) is the perturbed weight for event \\(i\\). \\(w_i\\) is the original weight for event \\(i\\). \\(SE_i\\) is the standard error of the weight. \\(c_i\\) is the half-width of the distribution, equal to \\(\\sqrt{6}\\,SE_i\\).\nFor example, with \\(w = 500\\) and \\(SE = 50\\), the half-width is \\(c = \\sqrt{6} \\times 50 \\approx 122.5\\). The perturbed weight is drawn from a triangular distribution over the interval \\([377.5, 622.5]\\), with the highest probability at 500. One simulation might draw \\(w^{*} = 560\\).", "section": "Triangular"}

**text**　輸出密度拉斯特參數值存儲模擬密度值的概要。 輸出密度計算法參數值決定了每個單元格如何對這些數值進行匯總。 默認平均值選項返回蒙特卡洛估計值( Efron和Tibshirani, 1993) 。 中位、 最小和最大選項返回每個單元格的相應摘要統計值。 平均選項計算主密度弧值如下: (\\\ large ~bar_lambda} =\ frac{1\\\\\\\\\B\\sumb=1\ _B}\lambda ⁇ b, s\\) 其中:\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\(B\) 是模擬數。\ (b\) 是模擬索引。\ (\\ lumbda\\\b,s\\) 是模擬中的單元格的密度 \\ (s\) 。

The Output Density Calculation Method parameter value determines how those values are summarized for each cell.\nThe default Mean option returns the Monte Carlo estimate (Efron and Tibshirani, 1993). The Median, Minimum, and Maximum options return the corresponding summary statistic for each cell.\nThe Mean option calculates the primary density raster as follows:\n\\(\\Large {\\bar{\\lambda}_s = \\frac{1}{B}\\sum_{b=1}^{B} \\lambda_{b,s}}\\)\nwhere: \\(\\bar{\\lambda}_s\\) is the mean density at cell \\(s\\). \\(B\\) is the number of simulations. \\(b\\) is the simulation index. \\(\\lambda_{b,s}\\) is the density at cell \\(s\\) in simulation \\(b\\).", "section": "Primary density raster"}

**text**　輸出標準偏移參數值存儲模擬密度值的變異性。 每個單元格模擬密度值的標準偏差使用以下公式計算( Welford 1962):\ (\ grand{SD_s =\ sqrt\ frac{1\\\B\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\是模擬的數量。\(\ grand\lambda ⁇ b,s ⁇ )是單元格的密度 \ (s\),重復\ (b\) 。下圖顯示標準偏差如何測量模擬密度值的分布。雖然右側的分布平均密度較大,但也有更大的標準偏差,表明各模擬之間變化更大。

The standard deviation of the simulated density values at each cell is calculated using the following formula (Welford 1962):\n\\(\\large{SD_s = \\sqrt{\\frac{1}{B-1}\\sum_{b=1}^{B}(\\lambda_{b,s} - \\bar{\\lambda}_s)^2}}\\)\nwhere: \\(SD_s\\) is the standard deviation of density at cell \\(s\\). \\(\\large \\bar{\\lambda}_s\\) is the mean density at cell \\(s\\). \\(B\\) is the number of simulations. \\(\\large \\lambda_{b,s}\\) is the density at cell \\(s\\), repetition \\(b\\).\nThe figure below illustrates how standard deviation measures the spread of simulated density values. Although the distribution on the right has a larger mean density, it also has a larger standard deviation, indicating greater variability across simulations.", "section": "Standard deviation raster"}

## 概率核心密度如何起作用 · 變化光柵的系數

**id**　ce6bb06cb37ddd43f1-32

**english**　How Probabilistic Kernel Density works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**text**　變換光柵參數值的輸出系數可以存儲密度相對于平均密度的可變性。為了確定每個單元格的變化系數,工具使用以下公式計算標準偏差與平均密度的比率(Efron和Tibshirani,1993年):\(大 CV_s=\LARGE\frac{SD_sthurbar\lambda ⁇ s}),其中:\(CV_s\)是單元格變量系數\(s\)。\(SD_s\)是單元格密度的標準偏差。\(\)\(大\bar~lambda}/)。\ (s\) 單元格的平均密度 \ (\\ max \ bbar_lambda\\\\ le 0\) , 以 NoData 處理為 NoData 。 例如, 一個平均密度為 5.0 和\ (SD= 0.5\) 的稠密單元格有\( CV = 0.10\) 。 一個平均密度為 0.2 和\ (SD = 0.1\) 的稀疏單元格有單元格有\ (CV = 01.0\) 。 雖然這個稀散單元格的標準偏差較小,但其密度估計值與其平均值相比比較是多變的。 下面的數字說明了變量系數(CV) 如何測量與平均密度相對的可變性。 盡管這個稀少的單元格標準偏差較小,但其密度估計值卻較小。由于平均密度要低得多,因此變化系數較高。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The Output Coefficient of Variation Raster parameter value stores the variability of density relative to the mean density. To determine the coefficient of variation at each cell, the tool calculates the ratio of the standard deviation to the mean density using the following formula (Efron and Tibshirani, 1993):\n\\(\\large CV_s = \\LARGE \\frac{SD_s}{\\bar{\\lambda}_s}\\)\nwhere: \\(CV_s\\) is the coefficient of variation at cell \\(s\\). \\(SD_s\\) is the standard deviation of density at cell \\(s\\). \\(\\large \\bar{\\lambda}_s\\) is the mean density at cell \\(s\\), with \\(\\large \\bar{\\lambda}_s \\le 0\\) treated as NoData.\nFor example, a dense cell with a mean density of 5.0 and \\(SD = 0.5\\) has \\(CV = 0.10\\). A sparse cell with a mean density of 0.2 and \\(SD = 0.1\\) has \\(CV = 0.50\\). Although the sparse cell has a smaller standard deviation, its density estimate is more variable relative to its mean.\nThe figure below illustrates how the coefficient of variation (CV) measures variability relative to the mean density. Although the sparse cell has a smaller standard deviation, its density estimate has a higher coefficient of variation because the mean density is much lower.", "section": "Coefficient of variation raster"}

## 概率核心密度如何起作用 · 超常概率光柵

**id**　ce6bb06cb37ddd43f1-33

**english**　How Probabilistic Kernel Density works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間分析員工具箱
- 密度工具
- 密度密度工具裝置概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#R31

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**text**　輸出超常概率光柵參數測量每個單元格密度超過特定閾值的概率。 它有助于確定各個模擬器( Efron 和 Tibshirani, 1993; Härdle 和 Marron, 1991)中密度始終高于決定閾值的地點( Efron 和 Tibshirani, 1993; Härdle 和 Marron, 1991)。 每個單元格的超常概率計算為密度值超過規定閾值的模擬比例,使用以下公式:\\\\ (大 P(\lambda_s > t) =\ frac{1\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\(P\lambda_s > t)\ 是單元格密度超過 \ (s\) 的概率。\ (B\) 是模擬數。\ (b\) 是模擬索引。\(b\) 是模擬中的單元格密度 \\\ (s\) 。\ (\ b\) 是模擬中的單元格密度 \ (s\) 。\ (t\) 是概率閾值 。\ (\\\\ mathbf{1}(\cdot)\ 是指示函數, 當條件為真實時等于 1, 否則等于 0 。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The Output Exceedance Probability Raster parameter measures the probability that density exceeds a specified threshold at each cell. It is useful for identifying locations where density is consistently above a decision threshold across simulations (Efron and Tibshirani, 1993; Härdle and Marron, 1991).\nThe exceedance probability at each cell is calculated as the proportion of simulations whose density values exceed the specified threshold using the following formula:\n\\(\\large P(\\lambda_s > t) = \\frac{1}{B}\\sum_{b=1}^{B} \\mathbf{1}(\\lambda_{b,s} > t)\\)\nwhere: \\(P(\\lambda_s > t)\\) is the probability that density at cell \\(s\\) exceeds \\(t\\). \\(B\\) is the number of simulations. \\(b\\) is the simulation index. \\(\\large \\lambda_{b,s}\\) is the density at cell \\(s\\) in simulation \\(b\\). \\(t\\) is the probability threshold. \\(\\mathbf{1}(\\cdot)\\) is the indicator function, equal to 1 when the condition is true and 0 otherwise.", "section": "Exceedance probability raster"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#R31

## 概率核心密度如何起作用 · 超常概率光柵

**id**　ce6bb06cb37ddd43f1-34

**english**　How Probabilistic Kernel Density works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間分析員工具箱
- 密度工具
- 密度密度工具裝置概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#R31

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**text**　下圖說明超常概率光柵是如何計算的。 概率是通過在特定閾值的單元格中比較模擬密度值和計算超過該閾值的模擬比重來確定的。 例如, 如果平均表面90百分位為0.30, 那么\( t=0.30\) 。 在200個模擬中的184個超過0.30的單元格中,超常概率是\( 184/ 200 = 0.92\) ; 在200個模擬中只有40個超過0.30的單元格中,超常概率是\( 40/ 200 = 0.20\) 。 將閾值提高到95百分位時,超常標準就更加嚴格了。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The figure below illustrates how the exceedance probability raster is calculated. The probability is determined by comparing the simulated density values at a cell with a specified threshold and calculating the proportion of simulations that exceed that threshold.\nFor example, if the 90th percentile of the mean surface is 0.30, then \\(t = 0.30\\). At a cell where 184 of 200 simulations exceeded 0.30, the exceedance probability is \\(184 / 200 = 0.92\\). At a cell where only 40 of 200 simulations exceed 0.30, the exceedance probability is \\(40 / 200 = 0.20\\). Increasing the threshold to the 95th percentile makes the exceedance criterion more stringent.", "section": "Exceedance probability raster"}

## 概率核心密度如何起作用 · 平板和大地測量方法對密度的影響

**id**　ce6bb06cb37ddd43f1-35

**english**　How Probabilistic Kernel Density works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間分析員工具箱
- 密度工具
- 密度密度工具裝置概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#32P

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**text**　計算輸出密度值取決于測量距離所使用的方法。 方法參數的平面選項適合于使用設計用于保持距離和面積的預測座標系統進行局部分析的當地分析。 默認情況下,工具使用平面法。 大地測量選項適合區域或全球分析, 包括在網絡Mercator或地理座標系統中進行的分析。 該方法用于計算地球曲線并正確處理極和國際日期線附近的資料。 平面和大地測量距離之間的差別與地理范圍和來源地點的距離不同。 對于大的研究地區,一般建議采用大地測量方法。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The calculation of output density values depends on the method used to measure distance. The Planar option of the Method parameter is appropriate for local analyses that use a projected coordinate system designed to preserve distance and area. By default, the tool uses a planar method.\nThe Geodesic option is appropriate for regional or global analyses, including analyses performed in a Web Mercator or geographic coordinate system. This method accounts for the curvature of the earth and correctly handles data near the poles and the international dateline.\nDifferences between planar and geodesic distance increase with geographic extent and distance from the source locations. For large study areas, the geodesic method is generally recommended.", "section": "Effect of planar and geodesic methods on density"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#32P

## 概率核心密度如何起作用 · 多重不確定性類型

**id**　ce6bb06cb37ddd43f1-42

**english**　How Probabilistic Kernel Density works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間分析員工具箱
- 密度工具
- 密度密度工具裝置概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#VTO

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**text**　當選擇多個不確定性類型時,它們之間的擾動在每次模擬中一起應用。 因此報告的不確定性反映了其綜合效應而不是任何單一來源。 要評估特定不確定性源的貢獻,請對每種類型分別運行工具并比較結果。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "When multiple uncertainty types are selected, their perturbations are applied together within each simulation. The reported uncertainty, therefore, reflects their combined effects rather than any single source. To evaluate the contribution of a specific uncertainty source, run the tool separately for each type, and compare the results.", "section": "Multiple uncertainty types"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#VTO

## 概率核心密度如何起作用 · 模擬太少

**id**　ce6bb06cb37ddd43f1-44

**english**　How Probabilistic Kernel Density works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間分析員工具箱
- 密度工具
- 密度密度工具裝置概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#D6R

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**text**　輸出弧度是根據有限的模擬數量估算的。 使用太少的模擬可以產生不穩定的平均值、標準偏差和概率估計。 增加模擬的數量一般會提高穩定性,但增加處理時間,特別是超常概率光柵的處理時間。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The output rasters are estimates based on a finite number of simulations. Using too few simulations can produce unstable mean, standard deviation, and probability estimates. Increasing the number of simulations generally improves stability but increases processing time, particularly for the exceedance probability raster.", "section": "Too few simulations"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#D6R

## 概率核心密度如何起作用 · 選擇分配方法

**id**　ce6bb06cb37ddd43f1-45

**english**　How Probabilistic Kernel Density works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 空間分析員工具箱
- 密度工具
- 密度密度工具裝置概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#QWR

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**text**　配送方法參數的邏輯正常、統一和三角分布選項保留相同的中值錯誤和標準差,但形狀不同。 邏輯正偏斜,可以產生偶發的大數值,而統一和三角是交界和對稱的。 對于絕對正數(如計數),請選擇最能反映資料預期范圍和變異性的分布。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The Lognormal, Uniform, and Triangular distribution options for the Distribution Method parameter preserve the same mean and standard error but differ in shape. Lognormal is positively skewed and can produce occasional large values, while Uniform and Triangular are bounded and symmetric. For strictly positive quantities such as counts, select the distribution that best reflects the expected range and variability of the data.", "section": "Choosing a distribution method"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#QWR

## 概率核心密度如何起作用 · 接近零密度變化的系數

- 工具參考

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**text**　當平均密度接近零時,變差系數會變得非常大。工具將 NoData 指派給平均密度為零或更低的單元格,但當平均密度非常小時,仍然可以出現極端值。解釋變差系數和平均密度光柵。

Interpret the coefficient of variation together with the mean density raster.", "section": "Coefficient of variation near zero density"}

## 概率核心密度如何起作用 · 解釋超常概率光柵

**id**　ce6bb06cb37ddd43f1-47

**english**　How Probabilistic Kernel Density works

- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具

- 密度工具

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html

**text**　超常概率光柵顯示在整個模擬中密度通常超過指定的閾值。 它不代表事件發生于某個地點的概率。 當報告結果時, 包括生成光柵所使用的閾值或百分位值 。

**original**　{"text": "The exceedance probability raster shows how often density exceeds the specified threshold across simulations. When reporting results, include the threshold or percentile used to generate the raster.", "section": "Interpreting the exceedance probability raster"}

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/how-probabilistic-kernel-density-works.html#GQC
